---
name: gitnexus-pr-review
description: "Use when the user wants to review a pull request, understand what a PR changes, assess risk of merging, or check for missing test coverage. Examples: \"Review this PR\", \"What does PR #42 change?\", \"Is this PR safe to merge?\""

# PR Review with GitNexus

## When to Use

- "Review this PR"
- "What does PR #42 change?"
- "Is this safe to merge?"
- "What's the blast radius of this PR?"
- "Are there missing tests for this PR?"
- Reviewing someone else's code changes before merge

## Workflow

```
1. gh pr diff <number>                                    → Get the raw diff
2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})  → Map diff to affected flows
3. For each changed symbol:
   gitnexus_impact({target: "<symbol>", direction: "upstream"})    → Blast radius per change
4. gitnexus_context({name: "<key symbol>"})               → Understand callers/callees
5. READ gitnexus://repo/{name}/processes                   → Check affected execution flows
6. Summarize findings with risk assessment
```

> If "Index is stale" → run `npx gitnexus analyze` in terminal before reviewing.

## Checklist

```
- [ ] Fetch PR diff (gh pr diff or git diff base...head)
- [ ] gitnexus_detect_changes to map changes to affected execution flows
- [ ] gitnexus_impact on each non-trivial changed symbol
- [ ] Review d=1 items (WILL BREAK) — are callers updated?
- [ ] gitnexus_context on key changed symbols to understand full picture
- [ ] Check if affected processes have test coverage
- [ ] Assess overall risk level
- [ ] Write review summary with findings
```

## Review Dimensions

| Dimension | How GitNexus Helps |
| --- | --- |
| **Correctness** | `context` shows callers — are they all compatible with the change? |
| **Blast radius** | `impact` shows d=1/d=2/d=3 dependents — anything missed? |
| **Completeness** | `detect_changes` shows all affected flows — are they all handled? |
| **Test coverage** | `impact({includeTests: true})` shows which tests touch changed code |
| **Breaking changes** | d=1 upstream items that aren't updated in the PR = potential breakage |

## Risk Assessment

| Signal | Risk |
| --- | --- |
| Changes touch <3 symbols, 0-1 processes | LOW |
| Changes touch 3-10 symbols, 2-5 processes | MEDIUM |
| Changes touch >10 symbols or many processes | HIGH |
| Changes touch auth, payments, or data integrity code | CRITICAL |
| d=1 callers exist outside the PR diff | Potential breakage — flag it |

## Tools

**gitnexus_detect_changes** — map PR diff to affected execution flows:

```
gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})

→ Changed: 8 symbols in 4 files
→ Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow, WebhookHandler
→ Risk: MEDIUM
```

**gitnexus_impact** — blast radius per changed symbol:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})

→ d=1 (WILL BREAK):
  - processCheckout (src/checkout.ts:42) [CALLS, 100%]
  - webhookHandler (src/webhooks.ts:15) [CALLS, 100%]

→ d=2 (LIKELY AFFECTED):
  - checkoutRouter (src/routes/checkout.ts:22) [CALLS, 95%]
```

**gitnexus_impact with tests** — check test coverage:

```
gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream", includeTests: true})

→ Tests that cover this symbol:
  - validatePayment.test.ts [direct]
  - checkout.integration.test.ts [via processCheckout]
```

**gitnexus_context** — understand a changed symbol's role:

```
gitnexus_context({name: "validatePayment"})

→ Incoming calls: processCheckout, webhookHandler
→ Outgoing calls: verifyCard, fetchRates
→ Processes: CheckoutFlow (step 3/7), RefundFlow (step 1/5)
```

## Example: "Review PR #42"

```
1. gh pr diff 42 > /tmp/pr42.diff
   → 4 files changed: payments.ts, checkout.ts, types.ts, utils.ts

2. gitnexus_detect_changes({scope: "compare", base_ref: "main"})
   → Changed symbols: validatePayment, PaymentInput, formatAmount
   → Affected processes: CheckoutFlow, RefundFlow
   → Risk: MEDIUM

3. gitnexus_impact({target: "validatePayment", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: processCheckout, webhookHandler (WILL BREAK)
   → webhookHandler is NOT in the PR diff — potential breakage!

4. gitnexus_impact({target: "PaymentInput", direction: "upstream"})
   → d=1: validatePayment (in PR), createPayment (NOT in PR)
   → createPayment uses the old PaymentInput shape — breaking change!

5. gitnexus_context({name: "formatAmount"})
   → Called by 12 functions — but change is backwards-compatible (added optional param)

6. Review summary:
   - MEDIUM risk — 3 changed symbols affect 2 execution flows
   - BUG: webhookHandler calls validatePayment but isn't updated for new signature
   - BUG: createPayment depends on PaymentInput type which changed
   - OK: formatAmount change is backwards-compatible
   - Tests: checkout.test.ts covers processCheckout path, but no webhook test
```

## Review Output Format

Structure your review as:

```markdown
## PR Review: <title>

**Risk: LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH / CRITICAL**

### Changes Summary
- <N> symbols changed across <M> files
- <P> execution flows affected

### Findings
1. **[severity]** Description of finding
   - Evidence from GitNexus tools
   - Affected callers/flows

### Missing Coverage
- Callers not updated in PR: ...
- Untested flows: ...

### Recommendation
APPROVE / REQUEST CHANGES / NEEDS DISCUSSION
```

+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **NO** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | API-token issuance (name + status only, tokens get full access) and the OAuth2 contract are fully specified with no source-IP registration or restriction step. +| **ApiPay** — backend calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) for DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning and the BYO-key fallback | **NO** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Authentication is fully specified as `Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)` with keys issued in the ApiPay dashboard. The entire docs corpus contains no IP-allowlist, IP-registration or caller-IP-restriction concept; the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A-record instructions pointing at ApiPay's own server. |
+| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **NO** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The complete calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL). Bot creation and authentication define no place to declare a caller IP, and the documented error model (400/401/403/404/408/429) has no origin/IP-denial class — a mandatory source-IP allowlist cannot be operated without such a mechanism. |
+| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. --- CHANGED FILE ---
     1	# Plexco — Setup dev · staging · production
     2	
     3	> **Version:** v2.1 · 2026-07-16
     4	> **Authority:** DD-04 = DigitalOcean co-location: App Platform + Managed PostgreSQL HA in Singapore,
     5	> connected through the `sgp1` VPC. The accepted record is
     6	> [`../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md`](../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md).
     7	> **Current repository state:** the local Docker Compose definition exists and validates; Docker-daemon
     8	> availability remains machine state and **must be checked before each run**. The daemon was started for the
     9	> 2026-07-16 closure and the canonical PostgreSQL/RLS/auth suite passed; the test container was then stopped.
    10	> The production DigitalOcean App Spec and deployment workflows are still an infra-slice deliverable.
    11	> `infra/fly.toml` is a bounded fallback artifact, not the production runbook.
    12	
    13	Plexco is a money-safe, multi-tenant system. Environment separation, DB-role separation, immutable promotion,
    14	and restore/failover drills are release gates rather than optional hardening.
    15	
    16	---
    17	
    18	## 0. Target environment model
    19	
    20	```text
    21	local development
    22	  docker compose + local PostgreSQL
    23	            │
    24	            │ build one immutable OCI image (release tag + verified digest)
    25	            ▼
    26	ephemeral staging on DigitalOcean
    27	  production-shaped App Platform components + separate Managed PostgreSQL
    28	  sgp App Platform ── private route ── sgp1 VPC
    29	            │
    30	            │ promote the exact tested image; do not rebuild
    31	            ▼
    32	production on DigitalOcean
    33	  2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker + Managed PostgreSQL primary/standby
    34	```
    35	
    36	| Environment | Purpose | Lifetime | Database | Public hostname |
    37	|---|---|---|---|---|
    38	| **local** | Fast development and focused tests | Persistent on developer machine | Local Docker PostgreSQL; disposable data | `localhost` / tunnel only when a webhook needs it |
    39	| **integration smoke** | Optional shared callback target while a channel integration is active | Minimal and explicitly owned | Separate non-production DB; never production data | Stable non-production domain if required |
    40	| **staging** | UAT, DAST, load/soak, failover, PITR and provider-exit rehearsal | Ephemeral 48–96h with TTL | Separate production-shaped DO Managed PG | Staging-only domain/callbacks |
    41	| **production** | Live users and real money data | Persistent | DO Managed PG primary + standby, HA/PITR | Production domain |
    42	
    43	Staging must exercise the same process groups, VPC path, DB roles, migration job, and reconnect behavior as
    44	production. “Production-shaped” does not mean always-on: IaC creates it for a release/drill and destroys it
    45	automatically after the TTL.
    46	
    47	---
    48	
    49	## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
    50	
    51	### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
    52	
    53	DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
    54	table below closes this gate with attributable evidence from current official provider documentation, one row
    55	per production dependency and its exact Plexco outbound call surface.
    56	
    57	| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
    58	|---|---|---|---|---|
    59	| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **NO** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | API-token issuance (name + status only, tokens get full access) and the OAuth2 contract are fully specified with no source-IP registration or restriction step. The docs' only IP-allowlist page lists SePay's own egress IPs for merchants to allowlist SePay→merchant webhook delivery — the reverse direction — so inbound webhook hardening has no outbound-caller counterpart. |
    60	| **ApiPay** — backend calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) for DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning and the BYO-key fallback | **NO** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Authentication is fully specified as `Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)` with keys issued in the ApiPay dashboard. The entire docs corpus contains no IP-allowlist, IP-registration or caller-IP-restriction concept; the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A-record instructions pointing at ApiPay's own server. |
    61	| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **NO** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The complete calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL). Bot creation and authentication define no place to declare a caller IP, and the documented error model (400/401/403/404/408/429) has no origin/IP-denial class — a mandatory source-IP allowlist cannot be operated without such a mechanism. |
    62	| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
    63	
    64	<!-- DD04_EGRESS_EVIDENCE_CLOSED -->
    65	
    66	**Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE** — all four rows are attributable and all are **NO**.
    67	
    68	- `DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` permits the App Platform + VPC green path to proceed to later **manual design review
    69	  only**; it does not by itself authorize the infra slice or any provisioning. The marker above proves
    70	  evidence completeness, not that the answer was favorable.
    71	- A `FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED` result would have blocked the DO App Platform + VPC green path and required a
    72	  separate fallback design: the bounded Fly+DO fallback or a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access
    73	  and controlled egress.
    74	- If a future provider or contract change turns any row **YES**, stop. Do not disable the private DB path
    75	  silently; re-open this gate and the fallback decision before touching the App Spec.
    76	
    77	### 1.2 Repository preconditions
    78	
    79	Before the infra slice runs:
    80	
    81	```bash
    82	test -f infra/Dockerfile
    83	test -f infra/entrypoint.sh
    84	test -f .github/workflows/ci.yml
    85	test -f infra/app.prod.yaml       # created and reviewed by the infra slice
    86	test -f infra/app.staging.yaml    # created and reviewed by the infra slice
    87	```
    88	
    89	If either App Spec is absent, this runbook is a deployment contract only: **do not improvise a console-only
    90	production setup**. IaC must remain the reproducible source.
    91	
    92	---
    93	
    94	## 2. DigitalOcean resource layout
    95	
    96	Use one DO team/account with separate staging and production resources, tags, secrets, domains, and budgets.
    97	Co-location is a failure-domain trade: off-provider backup and restore rehearsal remain mandatory.
    98	
    99	| Resource | Staging | Production |
   100	|---|---|---|
   101	| App Platform app | `plexco-staging` | `plexco-prod` |
   102	| App region | `sgp` | `sgp` |
   103	| VPC | dedicated staging VPC in `sgp1` | dedicated production VPC in `sgp1` |
   104	| PostgreSQL | temporary production-shaped cluster | 2 GiB primary + 2 GiB standby at Day 1 |
   105	| DB trusted sources | staging app private identity + controlled admin/drill path only | production app private identity only; temporary admin path time-boxed and audited |
   106	| Domains/callbacks | staging-only Google/Meta/Zalo/SePay configuration | production configuration |
   107	| Budget alerts | TTL + staging ceiling | compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup, KMS |
   108	
   109	Do not share a database, VPC, OAuth client, Messenger app/page token, Zalo bot token, webhook secret, KMS
   110	ciphertext, or session secret across staging and production.
   111	
   112	---
   113	
   114	## 3. One image, three App Platform components
   115	
   116	The App Specs deploy the same immutable image as three components. Use a unique release tag such as the git SHA,
   117	resolve and record its registry digest, and prohibit mutable `latest` promotion.
   118	
   119	| Component | Day-1 production shape | Command | DB identity | Scaling contract |
   120	|---|---:|---|---|---|
   121	| `web` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh web` | `app` pooled URL + `app` direct listener URL | Request-based RPS/p95 autoscaling is eligible on shared CPU; CPU autoscaling requires dedicated CPU |
   122	| `discovery` | `2 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb` | `infra/entrypoint.sh discovery` | `readonly` pooled URL on the primary at Day 1 | Bounded pool + timeout + CDN + circuit breaker; read-only node only after telemetry trigger |
   123	| `worker` | `1 × apps-s-1vcpu-1gb-fixed` | `infra/entrypoint.sh worker` | `app_worker` direct/session URL | One durable consumer at Day 1; add a second scalable worker only after idempotency and queue-lag gates pass |
   124	
   125	App Platform high availability for the HTTP paths requires at least two containers, hence `web=2` and
   240	| SePay/ApiPay | sandbox/test account | staging account | production accounts/webhooks |
   241	| PWA/API base | localhost/tunnel | staging domain | production domain |
   242	
   243	Webhook updates are idempotent deployment steps. Verify the registered callback after every domain/app change; do
   244	not rely on a manual checklist entry without provider-side evidence.
   245	
   246	---
   247	
   248	## 8. Rollback and recovery
   249	
   250	### Application rollback
   251	
   252	- Select the previously verified immutable release in the App Spec and reconcile the app.
   253	- Confirm `web`/`discovery` health, SSE re-subscription, worker queue lag and no duplicate money mutations.
   254	- If a forward migration has run, do not execute destructive down-migrations. Use expand/contract and forward-fix.
   255	
   256	### Database recovery
   257	
   258	- Provider PITR and HA are not sufficient alone. Maintain encrypted logical exports outside the DO account.
   259	- Quarterly, restore to vanilla PostgreSQL outside DigitalOcean and run schema/role/RLS/sequence checks plus
   260	  ledger, allocation, outbox and row-count reconciliation.
   261	- Rehearse the cross-provider flow and rollback semantics from DD-04 §7.4. Never dual-write money data during
   262	  migration and never point back to a stale source after the target has accepted writes.
   263	
   264	---
   265	
   266	## 9. Ephemeral staging lifecycle
   267	
   268	Every staging creation requires an owner, release/drill purpose, `expires_at`, and cost ceiling. The automation:
   269	
   270	1. creates the VPC/app/database from IaC;
   271	2. restores anonymized/representative data or seeds production-shaped fixtures;
   272	3. runs the selected gates;
   273	4. exports drill evidence;
   274	5. destroys the app, DB, VPC and temporary credentials on TTL;
   275	6. alerts and fails closed if deletion does not complete.
   276	
   277	Persistent callback smoke infrastructure, when genuinely required, is a separate minimal resource and must not
   278	silently become an always-on production mirror.
   279	
   280	---
   281	
   282	## 10. Production acceptance checklist
   283	
   284	This list mirrors the accepted record §7.6 and must be represented as executable checks/evidence in the infra
   285	slice:
   286	
   287	- [x] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence — §1.1, checked 2026-07-18, overall result
   288	      `DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE`.
   289	- [ ] App Platform `sgp` is attached to the intended `sgp1` VPC.
   290	- [ ] PostgreSQL trusted sources contain only required private identities.
   291	- [ ] Four DB roles and pooled/direct/listener URLs work over the private route with TLS verification.
   292	- [ ] Two-instance `web` and `discovery` pass rolling-deploy and health tests.
   293	- [ ] Discovery pool cap, `statement_timeout`, reserved money connection budget, CDN/rate limits and circuit breaker pass load tests.
   294	- [ ] Primary-failure drill proves DB failover and application reconnect.
   295	- [ ] SSE re-subscribes and pg-boss resumes without duplicate money mutations.
   296	- [ ] PITR and off-provider restores meet measured RPO/RTO and reconciliation gates.
   297	- [ ] KMS rotation and decrypt-failure paths pass.
   298	- [ ] Ephemeral staging reproduces from IaC and destroys itself on TTL.
   299	- [ ] Load evidence captures RPS/concurrency, DB CPU/RAM/connections/query time, queue lag, p95/p99, error rate, egress and projected cost.
   300	- [ ] Cross-provider rehearsal and asymmetric rollback pass DD-04 §7.4.
   301	- [ ] Budget alerts cover compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup and KMS.
   302	
   303	---
   304	
   305	## 11. Cost planning
   306	
   307	| Environment | Planning envelope |
   308	|---|---:|
   309	| Local/integration smoke | `$0–15/month`, depending on whether a shared public callback target is kept |
   310	| Ephemeral staging | `$8–18/month` average at 48–96h per release/drill |

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-129-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:130:- VPC attachment to the environment's `sgp1` VPC; no dedicated egress-IP block on the VPC path.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-131-- Set `TRUSTED_INGRESS_PROVIDER=digitalocean` on every public HTTP component. Application rate limits trust
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-132-  only DigitalOcean's overwritten `do-connecting-ip`; they never fall back to `X-Forwarded-For` or a header
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-133-  owned by another provider. The bounded Fly fallback must instead set `fly`; `direct` is local-only.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-134-- Health checks on `/healthz` for both HTTP services.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-135-- Separate component-level environment variables and secrets.
--
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-282-## 10. Production acceptance checklist
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-283-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-284-This list mirrors the accepted record §7.6 and must be represented as executable checks/evidence in the infra
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-285-slice:
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-286-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:287:- [x] Fixed-egress-IP gate closed with written evidence — §1.1, checked 2026-07-18, overall result
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:288:      `DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE`.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:289:- [ ] App Platform `sgp` is attached to the intended `sgp1` VPC.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-290-- [ ] PostgreSQL trusted sources contain only required private identities.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-291-- [ ] Four DB roles and pooled/direct/listener URLs work over the private route with TLS verification.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-294-- [ ] Primary-failure drill proves DB failover and application reconnect.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-300-- [ ] Cross-provider rehearsal and asymmetric rollback pass DD-04 §7.4.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-301-- [ ] Budget alerts cover compute, DB, transfer, logs, backup and KMS.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-323-## 12. Bounded Fly fallback
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-324-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-325-`infra/fly.toml` is retained only because DD-04 pre-authorizes fallback `(a)` when a production dependency proves
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:326:that fixed outbound IP is mandatory and App Platform+VPC cannot satisfy it.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-327-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-328-Activating fallback requires a recorded gate result and a separate reviewed App/DB network plan:
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-329-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:330:- compute on Fly with static egress;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-331-- DO PostgreSQL reached over public TLS with `sslmode=verify-full` and IP allowlist;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-332-- the same four DB roles, immutable image, migration-job boundary, backup/restore and acceptance gates;
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-333-- a new cost reconciliation before procurement.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-334-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-335-Do not use the old “three Fly apps + Fly Postgres” topology. If fallback is not activated, no production Fly
--
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-343-- Console-only production resources that IaC cannot reproduce.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-344-- Migration/owner credential in any runtime component.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-345-- Mutable image tags or rebuilding between staging and production.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-346-- Treating the HA standby as the discovery replica.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-347-- Buying a read-only node from host count rather than measured contention.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:348:- Disabling VPC to obtain a fixed egress IP without activating the approved fallback/design branch.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-349-- Treating provider PITR as the off-provider backup.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-350-- Leaving ephemeral staging alive without an owner and TTL.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-48-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-49-## 1. Hard gates before provisioning
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-50-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-51-### 1.1 Fixed outbound-IP gate — DD-04 §7.1
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-52-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:53:DigitalOcean App Platform cannot use VPC networking and dedicated public egress IPs at the same time. The
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-54-table below closes this gate with attributable evidence from current official provider documentation, one row
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-55-per production dependency and its exact Plexco outbound call surface.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-56-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-57-| Dependency — Plexco outbound call surface | Fixed source IP | Primary source | Checked | Why the source supports the conclusion |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-58-|---|---|---|---|---|
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:59:| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **NO** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | API-token issuance (name + status only, tokens get full access) and the OAuth2 contract are fully specified with no source-IP registration or restriction step. The docs' only IP-allowlist page lists SePay's own egress IPs for merchants to allowlist SePay→merchant webhook delivery — the reverse direction — so inbound webhook hardening has no outbound-caller counterpart. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-60-| **ApiPay** — backend calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) for DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning and the BYO-key fallback | **NO** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Authentication is fully specified as `Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)` with keys issued in the ApiPay dashboard. The entire docs corpus contains no IP-allowlist, IP-registration or caller-IP-restriction concept; the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A-record instructions pointing at ApiPay's own server. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-61-| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **NO** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The complete calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL). Bot creation and authentication define no place to declare a caller IP, and the documented error model (400/401/403/404/408/429) has no origin/IP-denial class — a mandatory source-IP allowlist cannot be operated without such a mechanism. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:62:| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-63-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-64-<!-- DD04_EGRESS_EVIDENCE_CLOSED -->
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-65-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:66:**Overall result: DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE** — all four rows are attributable and all are **NO**.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-67-
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:68:- `DO_VPC_COMPATIBLE` permits the App Platform + VPC green path to proceed to later **manual design review
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-69-  only**; it does not by itself authorize the infra slice or any provisioning. The marker above proves
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-70-  evidence completeness, not that the answer was favorable.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:71:- A `FIXED_EGRESS_REQUIRED` result would have blocked the DO App Platform + VPC green path and required a
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-72-  separate fallback design: the bounded Fly+DO fallback or a DO Droplet/DOKS substrate with private DB access
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:73:  and controlled egress.
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-74-- If a future provider or contract change turns any row **YES**, stop. Do not disable the private DB path
docs/standards/environments-setup.md-75-  silently; re-open this gate and the fallback decision before touching the App Spec.

docs/standards/environments-setup.md-126-`discovery=2`. The worker starts single by design; pg-boss durability is in PostgreSQL, not local disk.

docs/standards/decisions.md-141-  - **Self-host / Railway-HA** — biến thể self-host (Patroni…), **không** Day-1 (hệ tiền + solo dev): tiết kiệm hạ tầng bị xoá bởi chi phí lao động/rủi ro.

docs/standards/decisions.md-143-- **Tiêu chí:** auto-failover, SLA, posture network, RLS (Postgres core ở mọi provider), pooler/direct-conn cho pg-boss/`LISTEN`, chi phí *dự đoán được*, độ trễ Singapore, maintainability team nhỏ, **portability (chỉ Postgres thuần)**.

name: control-in-app-browser
description: "Control the in-app Browser for opening, navigating, inspecting visible or interactive page state, clicking, typing, screenshots, and local web testing. # Browser
## Stop: choose the right surface before any browser action
Explicit browser intent wins: if the user names the in-app browser or Chrome, or asks to open, show, or navigate to a page; inspect its visual or interactive state; or interact with its UI, continue with Browser and do not substitute a connector.

Otherwise, treat a URL or open browser tab as context, not browser intent. Earlier Browser use does not make later semantic work browser-first. Before each semantic operation on a linked resource, you MUST query available and deferred tools for an applicable connector, API, or CLI. Reading this skill or scanning visible tools does not count. Do not initialize Browser for that operation until the query is complete. Use the non-browser tool when available. If it handles the current operation, continue the larger workflow without Browser for that operation. Use Browser when no such tool exists, the tool cannot access the resource or lacks a required capability, or UI work remains; use available browser context before asking the user to repeat it.

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

## Setup Documentation
Use `await agent.documentation.get("<name>")` when one of these setup topics applies:
- `bootstrap-troubleshooting`: read when browser setup succeeds but discovery or selection fails
- `chrome-troubleshooting`: read when Chrome extension setup, installation, or communication fails

## Bootstrap
These setup details are internal. User-facing progress updates should be less technical in nature. Never mention `Node REPL`, `node_repl`, `REPL`, JavaScript sessions, module exports, reading documentation, or loading instructions unless a user is asking for that exact information. If setup or recovery is needed, describe it naturally as connecting to the browser or retrying the browser connection.

The `browser-client` module is the core entry point for browser use, and is available under `scripts/browser-client.mjs` in this plugin's root directory. ALWAYS import it using an absolute path. IMPORTANT: If this path cannot be found, stop and report that this plugin is missing `scripts/browser-client.mjs`. NEVER use the built in `browser-client` library.

Run browser setup code through the Node REPL `js` tool. In this environment the callable tool id typically appears as `mcp__node_repl__js`. If it is not already available, use tool discovery for `node_repl js` without setting a result limit. You need the `js` execution tool: `js_reset` only clears state, and `js_add_node_module_dir` only changes package resolution. Do not call either helper while trying to expose `js`. If `js` is still not available, search again for `node_repl js` with `limit: 10`.

Initialize the runtime once per fresh Node session. If `agent.browsers` already exists, reuse it; do not import or initialize another browser runtime.

```js
if (globalThis.agent?.browsers == null) {
  const { setupBrowserRuntime } = await import("<plugin root>/scripts/browser-client.mjs");
  await setupBrowserRuntime({ globals: globalThis });
}
```

Once a browser connection is established, reuse its existing browser binding across later turns and do not reread this skill. Once you have read a browser's complete documentation, do not read it again unless you select a different browser.

A tab binding is separate from its browser binding. If a later turn reports that a tab is missing, stale, closed, or not part of the current browser session, discard that tab binding and obtain or create a fresh tab from the existing browser binding. An empty `browser.tabs.list()` or `browser.user.openTabs()` result is normal after tab cleanup and does not invalidate the browser binding. Never call `agent.browsers.get*` to recover a tab; only an explicit browser-disconnected error invalidates the binding.

## Browser selection
The scenarios below are for the initial browser selection only. Before calling any `agent.browsers.get*` method, reuse an existing `globalThis.browser`, `globalThis.iab`, or `globalThis.chrome` binding that already serves the task. A new user turn does not invalidate a browser binding or require another selection or documentation call.

Select the initial browser with exactly one of these scenarios, in the order
shown. An explicit request for the in-app browser or Chrome always wins over URL
selection. Never call `getForUrl()` when the user names a browser.

App-provided in-app-browser context is ambient UI state, not a user instruction to select or switch browsers. Only the text of the user's request can explicitly choose a browser.

Do not inspect browser cookies, local storage, profiles, passwords, or session stores. Browser discovery must remain read-only.

When authentication blocks requested browser navigation, do not replace it with web search, a search engine, another site, or another source merely to bypass sign-in.

### The user explicitly requests a browser
A plugin mention in the user's request explicitly names its browser.
`[@Browser](plugin://browser@openai-bundled)` names the in-app browser.
`[@Chrome](plugin://chrome@openai-bundled)`,
`[@chrome-internal](plugin://chrome-internal@openai-bundled)`, and
`[@chrome-dev](plugin://chrome-dev@openai-bundled)` name Chrome. Follow the
corresponding explicit-browser scenario below.

The in-app browser is available only when the Browser skill is listed for the session. If the user explicitly requests the in-app browser and that skill is available, use a distinct persistent binding and immediately read its complete documentation:

```js
if (globalThis.iab == null) {
  globalThis.iab = await agent.browsers.get("iab");
  nodeRepl.write(await iab.documentation());
}
```

If the user explicitly requests the in-app browser but the Browser skill is not available, report that the in-app browser is unavailable instead of substituting another browser.

Chrome is available only when the Chrome skill is listed for the session. If the user explicitly requests Chrome and that skill is available, use a separate persistent binding and immediately read its complete documentation:

```js
if (globalThis.chrome == null) {
  globalThis.chrome = await agent.browsers.get("extension");
  nodeRepl.write(await chrome.documentation());
}
```

If the user explicitly requests Chrome but the Chrome skill is not available, report that Chrome is unavailable instead of substituting another browser.

An explicit browser choice remains in force for the task. If authentication blocks the task in an explicitly selected browser, your next response must explicitly ask the user to sign in in that browser and tell you when it is ready, unless that browser's documentation provides a supported authentication flow to try first. Merely reporting that sign-in is required is not sufficient. Do not switch to another browser unless the user asks or approves the switch.

### The task requires browser interaction, the user does not specify a browser, and the task has a target URL
When the user supplies a URL or the intended URL can be reasonably inferred from the request, replace the example below with that URL and let browser-client choose the browser best suited to it:

```js
if (globalThis.browser == null) {
  globalThis.browser = await agent.browsers.getForUrl("https://example.com/");
  nodeRepl.write(await browser.documentation());
}
```

### The user specifies neither a browser nor a target URL
Use the runtime default, which prefers the in-app browser when it is available and otherwise uses Chrome. Do not list browsers first:

```js
if (globalThis.browser == null) {
  globalThis.browser = await agent.browsers.getDefault();
  nodeRepl.write(await browser.documentation());
}
```

## After setup
If setup succeeds but browser discovery or selection fails, read `await agent.documentation.get("bootstrap-troubleshooting")` before resetting the JavaScript session or trying another browser-control mechanism.

If the failure is specific to Chrome extension setup, installation, or communication, read `await agent.documentation.get("chrome-troubleshooting")` before retrying or taking another recovery action.

When the user did not explicitly choose a browser, a browser selected by the runtime is not a user constraint. Do not switch browsers based only on an assumption about authentication. If navigation shows that the selected browser lacks the required authentication, select another available browser before asking the user to sign in. You may select it without resetting the Node session. Preserve existing `iab`, `chrome`, and `browser` bindings when they are still useful. Existing tabs remain bound to the browser that created them. After selecting a different browser, obtain a tab from that browser before continuing and read its complete documentation.

The ability to interact directly with browsers is exposed through the `browser-client` runtime via the `agent.browsers.*` API. Before trying to interact with a selected browser for the first time, you MUST emit and read the complete documentation returned by its `documentation()` call in one go. For the initial documentation read, run the exact direct `nodeRepl.write(await <browser>.documentation());` call shown in the applicable scenario above. Do not assign the documentation to a variable, inspect its length, slice it, truncate it, summarize it, or emit only an excerpt. Do not proactively split the documentation into pages or chunks. Only if the tool output itself explicitly reports that it was truncated may you emit and read smaller chunks until you have read the documentation in its entirety.

Only the Node REPL `js` tool (`mcp__node_repl__js`) can be used to control the selected browser. Do not use external MCP browser-control tools, separate browser automation servers, or other browser skills for this surface. References to Playwright mean the in-skill `tab.playwright` API after browser-client setup.

# DD-04 was resolved in favor of DigitalOcean co-location and propagated across the Plexco repository.

Rollout context: The user asked which of two DD-04 options maximizes project benefit. The agent reviewed the decision brief, canonical infrastructure/decision docs, repository topology, current vendor documentation, and deployment dependencies, then updated dependent artifacts.

## Task 1: Decide DD-04 hosting/network posture

Outcome: success

Key steps:
- Reopened `docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md`, `docs/standards/decisions.md`, and `docs/standards/deployment-infra.md` as canonical anchors.
- Verified vendor facts for DigitalOcean Managed PostgreSQL, Neon, Supabase, Fly.io, and DigitalOcean App Platform.
- Confirmed that Fly private networking does not make an external managed DB private; true private networking requires PrivateLink or co-location.
- Assessed code/topology constraints: Postgres HA + PITR, Singapore region, `LISTEN/NOTIFY`, pg-boss direct/session connections, RLS, least-privilege DB roles, and public discovery isolation.
- Final decision: **QĐ-1(c) co-locate compute and DB on DigitalOcean**—DO App Platform plus DO Managed PostgreSQL HA standby in the same private VPC, Singapore. DD-10 remains standalone-container architecture; only the deployment substrate changes from Fly to DO.
- Fly remains a bounded fallback using public TLS, `sslmode=verify-full`, static egress IPs, and trusted-source allowlisting if the egress-IP gate fails.

Reusable knowledge:
- DO Managed PostgreSQL with at least one standby has automatic failover and a documented 99.95% SLA; no-standby clusters have lower SLA.
- DO App Platform supports web services, workers, internal services, health checks, autoscaling, Singapore availability, and VPC connectivity to DO resources.
- App Platform VPC and dedicated public egress IPs cannot be used simultaneously, so fixed-outbound-IP requirements for SePay/ApiPay/Zalo/Messenger must be verified before provisioning. Failure must trigger the documented Fly fallback rather than silently disabling VPC.
- Day-1 topology uses read isolation on the primary: projection-only `readonly` role, bounded pool, statement timeout, reserved money-path connections, CDN/rate limiting/circuit breaker. A physical read-only node is trigger-based, not mandatory Day-1.
- Portability is more than changing `DATABASE_URL`: use an immutable OCI image and require migration/rehearsal, logical replication/cutover, sequence/ledger reconciliation, and performance gates.

Failures and how to do differently:
- Full DB tests were not executable because local PostgreSQL/Docker was unavailable (`ECONNREFUSED ::1:5432`, `127.0.0.1:5432`; Docker daemon unavailable). Do not report DB assertions as passing; rerun with the repository’s PostgreSQL service enabled.
- One cleanup attempt using `rm -rf` was rejected by the shell safety policy; explicit `unlink` plus `rmdir` succeeded.
- A stale onboarding version string (`v0.4.8`) was found and corrected to `v0.6.0`.

docs/dd-14-15-zalo-spike-analysis.md:30:  - **Độc lập** → có thể bật Zalo Login **trước** OA → làm cầu **auto-match "Zalo login ↔ Zalo bot"** (giảm friction, thay SĐT). Ảnh hưởng **DD-32/provider-link path** (KHÔNG phải DD-13 — đã fence Google/email).

docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md-26-
docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md-27-## 2. DD-34 — Global KB plane (contract + guards)
docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md-28-- **Schema:** `platform_kb_sources/documents/chunks` — **no `tenant_id`**; role `app` **SELECT only**; ghi bởi role **platform-admin**. **KHÔNG** vào RLS list `0009` (như `public_sessions`).
docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md-29-- **Union retrieval:** `searchKnowledgeBase(tenantCtx, query)` = `kb_chunks WHERE tenant (RLS)` ∪ `platform_kb_chunks (global)`, hợp nhất **RRF**, gắn nhãn scope; **precedence club > platform** (`bot_configs.include_platform_kb`/`platform_kb_weight`).
docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md:30:- **Guards (bảng SELECT-mọi-tenant → phải chặt):** thiếu `tenant_id` chỉ ngăn routing, **KHÔNG** tự ngăn ingest nhầm tenant-data. ⇒ (1) **source allowlist** — chỉ ingest platform-docs-only; (2) **admin review** trước `status='active'`; (3) **scanner + test bất biến** assert `content` không chứa tenant identifier/PII (SĐT/email/`pay_code`/tên roster).
docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md-31-- **Quan hệ DD-21 (extension):** mở **allowed KB sources** từ `kb_chunks(tenant)` → `∪ platform_kb_chunks`; grounding vẫn **chỉ KB**; live-context (DD-28) vẫn tách. Patch DD-21 khi ✅.
docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md-32-
docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md-33-## 3. DD-35 — Auto-derive KB-club (contract + ranh giới)
docs/dd-34-36-kb-platform-analysis.md-34-- `kb_sources.kind='derived'`; 1 aggregate canonical : 1 derived source (upsert theo `derive_key`), idempotent `content_hash`.

docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-97-### 3.4 Realtime + push (background)
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-98-
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-99-- **Foreground:** SSE DD-07; PWA dùng `EventSource`, RN dùng client streaming phù hợp. Topic luôn server-derived theo tenant/person và re-authorize theo contract SSE hiện hành.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-100-- **Background:** APNs/FCM (Expo Push hoặc direct provider) qua **`device_push_subscriptions` global-protected**, gắn `person_id + app_instance_id`, endpoint lưu encrypted + lookup hash, lifecycle `active|revoked|invalid`, không `tenant_id` authority.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md:101:- **Không dùng `ChannelIdentity` cho device:** DD-67 `channel_identities/channel_reachability` tiếp tục chỉ model exact Messenger Page / Zalo bot-OA endpoint. Push subscription và bot reachability là hai protected stores; store này không authorize store kia.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-102-- Dispatcher re-check recipient eligibility cho target event/tenant, rồi áp notification policy. DD-67 chọn một exact channel route trong các Page/bot candidates; push route lấy từ device store. Logical delivery key ngăn accidental duplicate. Nếu product cố ý fan-out nhiều device thì delivery key phải bind từng subscription; đó là policy rõ, không phải broadcast do identity collision.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-103-- **Push payload tối thiểu:** notification id/type + generic copy + protected deep link. Không amount, debt, payer/player name, roster, venue riêng tư hoặc token trong lock-screen payload; app mở rồi fetch DTO sau auth.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-104-
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-105-### 3.5 Idempotency & offline
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-106-
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-107-- `processed_commands(tenant_id,command_id)` **đã có ở schema** nhưng chỉ dành cho tenant-domain mutation và còn phải được service wiring theo feature. Replay cùng `command_id` phải khớp `command_type + canonical payload hash`; collision khác payload fail closed.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-108-- Auth/refresh/logout, identity/link/merge và device-subscription lifecycle là global/protected flow; dùng conditional consume, unique key, family revoke và state-idempotency riêng — không ép vào tenant `processed_commands`.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-109-- **Native v1 offline = read-only:** chỉ cache DTO được whitelist; không cache tiền/PII. Không queue join/leave/pay/mark-paid/merge/admin/session mutation. Khi offline hiển thị trạng thái rõ và bắt refresh trước action.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md:110:- Sau này chỉ mở offline mutation cho allowlist low-risk có `expectedVersion`, expiry, re-authorization/revalidation tại consume và UX conflict rõ. Chat online retry có thể dùng `client_msg_id` dedupe theo DD-38.
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-111-
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-112-### 3.6 Versioning & 3 tầng
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-113-
docs/dd-69-app-native-analysis.md-114-- `/api/v1/...` là **target contract chưa live**; route hiện tại vẫn chủ yếu `/api/auth/*` và `/api/onboarding/*`. Trước native phải inventory route, pin v1 DTO/envelope/auth legend và có compatibility path để PWA không đổi hành vi.

docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-123-## 4. Process model & DB role
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-124-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-125-| Process | Vai trò | DB role | Connection | Ingress | Scale |
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-126-|---|---|---|---|---|---|
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md:127:| **web** | dashboard, API, SSE, **mọi webhook** (Zalo/Messenger/SePay/ApiPay) | `app` (NOSUPERUSER NOBYPASSRLS) | **pooled** (txn) cho request + **1 direct (session)** cho SSE `LISTEN` (`DATABASE_URL_LISTEN`) | HTTP :3000 | ngang; RPS/p95 autoscale dùng shared hoặc dedicated, CPU autoscale cần dedicated CPU |
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-128-| **discovery** | chỉ `GET /discover` (cross-tenant, đọc **projection công khai**) | `readonly` (**chỉ SELECT trên `public_sessions` + public view**) | **pooled, bounded** trên primary Day-1; đổi riêng component `DATABASE_URL` sang read-only node khi trigger | HTTP (route con) | ngang + CDN cache |
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-129-| **worker** | pg-boss scheduler/dispatcher, projectors, cron nhắc nợ | `app_worker` (chỉ narrow functions) | **DIRECT** (session-mode) | **không** ingress | 1 → vài replica (claim `SKIP LOCKED`) |
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-130-
docs/standards/deployment-infra.md-131-> **Vì sao tách `discovery` ra process riêng** (dù cùng image): đây là đường đọc *cố ý cross-tenant*, *không PII*, và *chịu DDoS cao nhất* (G6). Tách process + DB role cho phép áp **pool/timeout/circuit-breaker + CDN/rate-limit riêng** ngay Day-1; khi telemetry chạm trigger, chỉ đổi connection của component này sang read-only node, không đổi code hay API. Nếu sau này phải "tách 1 thứ" thành service thật, đây là ứng viên duy nhất hợp lý.

docs/standards/decisions.md-100-  - **DB lưu suffix trần UPPERCASE, 4 ký tự**, alphabet **A-Z + 2-9 trừ `0 O 1 I L`** (31, không phải Crockford); unique per-tenant; **immutable**; `CHECK (format + ≥1 chữ cái, loại toàn số)`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-101-  - **Prefix = bảng global `pay_prefix_registry(prefix PK, tenant_id, state current|retired, locked_at)` — nguồn DUY NHẤT, KHÔNG cột `tenants.pay_prefix`.** `UNIQUE one-current-per-tenant`; retired KHÔNG tái dụng (PK cho 1 tx thắng). **MVP: prefix IMMUTABLE vĩnh viễn sau code đầu tiên** (bỏ controlled-change — `memberships` chỉ lưu suffix nên đổi prefix sẽ resolve nhầm; tương lai cần thì phải có mapping `(membership_id,prefix,suffix,issued_at)`). **Choke-point atomic** phát-code ↔ đổi-prefix (`SELECT current-prefix FOR UPDATE` cùng lock order). Memo/QR `<PREFIX>-<CODE>`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-102-  - **Recognition = resolver `own | foreign | none`** (narrow SECURITY DEFINER, fail-closed — role `app` không blanket cross-tenant, `0009`). **full-issued ⟺ `registry.locked_at IS NOT NULL` + `membership.pay_code` khớp** (retired-chưa-lock + suffix mới → `none`, không false-issued). Prefix BẮT BUỘC; regex thêm `-?`. **Memo-level precedence:** hard-token (foreign / own-prefix+unissued / dash-lạ / error / >1-candidate) → HARD unmatched (thắng name-fallback); ≥2 distinct own → unmatched; đúng 1 own → resolve; chỉ `none`(`PAYMENT`) → name-fallback.
docs/standards/decisions.md-103-  - **Allocation = INVARIANT** qua **`planIncomingAllocations()`** (pay_code chỉ resolve MEMBERSHIP; ledger quyết charge): auto `exact`/`over`/`under`(FIFO partial, edge #8) **+ unknown-fee-phía-sau (giữ phần chắc chắn)**; manual **unknown-fee-đầu**/`already-paid`; ≥2 code / provider-code-mismatch → unmatched.
docs/standards/decisions.md:104:  - **Provider-agnostic:** parser/generator/matcher ở core. **ApiPay = Phase-1 primary** (DD-30) — parse `data.content` (schema reverify khi build); **SePay = fallback**, `code` chỉ evidence; giữ webhook nhận tx không-code (không `skip_if_no_code`).
docs/standards/decisions.md-105-  - **Merge:** pay_code giữ trên tombstone; recognition **follow `merged_into_membership_id`** (tenant/status/cycle guards). `left`-còn-nợ nhận diện.
docs/standards/decisions.md:106:- **Propagation ĐÃ làm ở SPEC (2026-07-13):** `identity-model` §merge + `feature_identity_tech` (pay_code tombstone follow-through) ✅ · connector `feature_connector_onboarding.md` 4.3b + `_tech` M2 (ApiPay enterprise = Phase-1 **primary**) + **schema `provider_connections.auth_mode += 'enterprise'`** (CHECK shape đầy đủ per provider/auth_mode; platform credential từ KMS, không per-tenant) + **CO-OQ-6 đóng** (`skip_if_no_code=0`, adapter-only) ✅ · `feature_payment_reconcile_tech` **§4 worker pipeline** (`normalize→discover→resolve→precedence→providerCode-crosscheck→plan`) + `bank_transactions.provider_code_enc` + §8 fixtures ✅.
docs/standards/decisions.md-107-- **Propagation CÒN LẠI (khi build P4, code):** wire core + **thay tests** (impact analysis trước khi sửa `matching.ts`/`payments-logic.ts`); implement **`discoverPayCodeCandidates` + resolver narrow fn + `planIncomingAllocations()`**; migration **`pay_prefix_registry`** (one-current-per-tenant, choke-point) + `CHECK` trên `memberships.pay_code`.

docs/standards/decisions.md-123-## DD-04 — Hosting PostgreSQL · ✅ Accepted (2026-07-16) — Option (c) DO co-locate
docs/standards/decisions.md-124-
docs/standards/decisions.md-125-> ✅ **FINAL (2026-07-16, owner):** chọn **QĐ-1 (c) — co-locate compute + Postgres trên DigitalOcean** (App Platform + Managed PG HA-standby, **cùng VPC private**, Singapore). Supersede đề xuất (a) trong brief. Đổi deployment target **Fly → DO** (KHÔNG đảo DD-10 — App Platform chạy standalone container). Lý do: private DB by construction + managed HA/SLA/PITR + một provider (đơn giản cho solo dev) + fit `LISTEN/NOTIFY` session-pool (không scale-to-zero) + portability. Record đầy đủ: `../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md`.
docs/standards/decisions.md-126->
docs/standards/decisions.md:127:> ⚠️ **Pre-build gate (§7.1) — CHƯA verify:** DO App Platform **không bật đồng thời** VPC-private-DB **và** dedicated egress-IP. **Phải xác minh** SePay/ApiPay/Zalo/Messenger có yêu cầu fixed outbound source-IP không **TRƯỚC khi dựng DO App Spec**; nếu có → dùng fallback **(a) Fly + DO**, **KHÔNG** âm thầm tắt private-DB path. Mitigation bắt buộc: **offsite encrypted backup + restore-to-another-Postgres runbook** (chống concentration risk). Cost planning (record Rev 2 — Day-1 baseline + trigger-based): **`$120–135/mo` Day-1 baseline** (2 web + 2 discovery + 1 worker × 1 GiB + DO PG HA `$60.90` + Cloudflare Free + Sentry Developer + KMS `$1–3` + logs/R2 `$0–5` + egress `$0–8`) — **money-safe nhờ non-negotiables + read-isolation controls** (projection-role, bounded pool, reserved budget, CDN, circuit-breaker), KHÔNG cần read-replica/WAF-Pro/Sentry-Team Day-1. Add-on đó = **trigger-based upgrade** khi telemetry chạm ngưỡng. Ephemeral staging `$8–18` → prod+staging `$128–153`. **Caveat:** deferral chỉ an toàn khi read-isolation controls ship thật + trigger được giám sát. (thay số Tier `$202–254` cũ; chi tiết deployment-infra §9 + record §5)

docs/standards/decisions.md-132-
docs/standards/decisions.md-133-- **Bối cảnh:** Cần chọn nhà cung cấp Postgres cho hệ tiền multi-tenant RLS; compute app ban đầu target Fly.io — **DD-04 chốt (c) → DO App Platform** (DD-10 giữ nguyên: standalone container).
docs/standards/decisions.md-134-- **Đã chốt:** Managed Postgres **HA (auto-failover) + PITR**, **region Singapore**, **private VPC** (co-locate). Railway PG chỉ dev/staging.
docs/standards/decisions.md-135-- **Gate cứng (đã đạt qua (c)):** **auto-failover HA + uptime SLA + posture private-network** (VPC by construction).
docs/standards/decisions.md:136:- **QĐ-1 — posture network:** (a) public TLS + `sslmode=verify-full` + static-egress-IP allowlist [giữ Fly — **fallback**] · (b) PrivateLink AWS [rời Fly — loại] · **(c) co-locate compute+DB cùng cloud [đổi target Fly→DO, KHÔNG đảo DD-10] — ✅ ĐÃ CHỌN**.
docs/standards/decisions.md-137-- **QĐ-2 — ứng viên (theo posture a):**
docs/standards/decisions.md-138-  - **DigitalOcean Managed [nghiêng chọn]** — auto-failover standby + SLA 99.95% + PITR; bill phẳng, vận hành đơn giản; margin dự đoán chắc nhất (không nhất thiết rẻ nhất).
docs/standards/decisions.md-139-  - **Neon Scale** — HA multi-AZ **mọi plan**; SLA + PrivateLink ở Scale (PrivateLink **AWS-only → vô dụng từ Fly**); consumption floor 0.25 CU, trần = max-CU; rẻ hơn ở tải thấp nhưng cần benchmark.
docs/standards/decisions.md-140-  - **Supabase** — Pro và Team **rớt uptime-SLA gate**. Team từ `$599` có AWS PrivateLink nhưng không uptime SLA; SLA chỉ ở Enterprise custom, còn auto-failover phải contract-confirm. Không cost-comparable với finalist Day-1.
docs/standards/decisions.md-141-  - **Self-host / Railway-HA** — biến thể self-host (Patroni…), **không** Day-1 (hệ tiền + solo dev): tiết kiệm hạ tầng bị xoá bởi chi phí lao động/rủi ro.
docs/standards/decisions.md-142-- **Sửa dữ kiện so với lần thu hẹp trước:** (1) Neon HA **không** Scale-gated (multi-AZ mọi plan; Scale = SLA/PrivateLink/IP-allow); (2) Supabase Pro/Team không có uptime SLA; Team từ `$599` chỉ thêm PrivateLink, SLA ở Enterprise custom và auto-failover cần contract confirmation; (3) private-networking **chưa** được giải quyết bởi "Fly private networking" (khác cloud).
docs/standards/decisions.md-143-- **Tiêu chí:** auto-failover, SLA, posture network, RLS (Postgres core ở mọi provider), pooler/direct-conn cho pg-boss/`LISTEN`, chi phí *dự đoán được*, độ trễ Singapore, maintainability team nhỏ, **portability (chỉ Postgres thuần)**.
docs/standards/decisions.md-144-- **Hệ quả:** RPO/RTO chốt sau provision + restore drill; benchmark tải trước khi cam kết chi phí (đặc biệt Neon consumption). Không đổi domain model.
docs/standards/decisions.md:145:- **✅ Đã chốt (2026-07-16):** QĐ-1 = **(c) co-locate DO**; QĐ-2 = **DigitalOcean** (App Platform + Managed PG). **Còn lại TRƯỚC khi build infra slice:** (1) **§7.1 egress-IP gate** (verify SePay/ApiPay/Zalo/Messenger fixed-outbound-IP); (2) production acceptance **§7.6** (auto-failover drill · PITR restore drill · 4 DB roles · SSE reconnect · pg-boss no-dup · budget alerts); (3) benchmark cost ở staging trước procurement.
docs/standards/decisions.md-146-- **Chi tiết:** **`../dd-04-hosting-final-decision.md` (final ✅)** · `../dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md` (rev 5, phân tích/nguồn) · `deployment-infra.md §2/§6/§9 (DD-04)`.

docs/standards/decisions.md-232-
docs/standards/decisions.md:233:- **Bối cảnh:** Zalo có nhiều bề mặt tách biệt (Bot Open API, Mini App, OA OpenAPI, Social/Login). Chiến lược auto-match "Zalo login ↔ Zalo bot" (giảm friction, thay cho SĐT) phụ thuộc việc bật được Zalo Login.
docs/standards/decisions.md-234-- **Cần xác minh:** Zalo Login yêu cầu đăng ký app trên developers.zalo.me — có buộc phải có OA verified không (thứ đang pending), hay độc lập.
docs/standards/decisions.md-235-- **Hệ quả:** Nếu độc lập → có thể bật Zalo Login trước OA, dùng làm cầu nối auto-match. Ảnh hưởng **DD-32 / provider-link path** (KHÔNG phải DD-13 — đã fence Google/email) và thiết kế link (`00-start-here/identity-model.md §5 link-code contract · §7 provider/channel phase matrix`).
docs/standards/decisions.md-236-
docs/standards/decisions.md:237:## DD-15 — Zalo Bot `from.id` có ổn định xuyên nhiều bot khác nhau không · ⬜ Mở — SPIKE (2026-07-13) — `../dd-14-15-zalo-spike-analysis.md`
docs/standards/decisions.md-238-> **Spike xác minh Zalo** (gộp với DD-14). Không chặn an toàn identity: target luôn scope theo bot/OA endpoint cho tới khi bằng chứng provider cho phép nới. Kết quả spike chỉ có thể giảm friction/linking, không được làm điều kiện để tránh collision.
docs/standards/decisions.md-239-
docs/standards/decisions.md-240-- **Bối cảnh:** Plexco đa-bot (mỗi host/CLB một bot). Cùng một người ở hai bot khác nhau: `from.id` là id toàn cục theo user Zalo, hay scoped-per-bot? (Messenger PSID đã xác nhận page-scoped.)
docs/standards/decisions.md:241:- **Cần xác minh:** với Zalo Bot Platform.
docs/standards/decisions.md-242-- **Target fail-safe hiện hành:** normalize hai keyed HMAC domain-separated: `provider_scope_lookup_hash = HMAC("channel-scope:zalo:<bot_or_oa_id>")` và `platform_user_lookup_hash = HMAC("channel-user:zalo:<from.id>")`; uniqueness là `(platform, provider_scope_lookup_hash, platform_user_lookup_hash)`. Raw bot/OA id và `from.id` không trở thành authority key/plaintext lookup.
docs/standards/decisions.md-243-- **Hệ quả:** Nếu sau này chứng minh `from.id` ổn định xuyên bot, một migration/merge-plan được audit có thể giảm số lần link; không tự đổi uniqueness hoặc auto-merge bằng suy đoán. Nếu scoped-per-bot thì contract hiện tại đã đúng. `link_codes` bao ca xấu nhất (`00-start-here/identity-model.md §3`).

docs/standards/decisions.md:359:## DD-30 — Provider onboarding: APIpay enterprise auto-provision per-host · ✅ Chốt
docs/standards/decisions.md-360-
docs/standards/decisions.md:361:- **Bối cảnh:** Onboarding hiện tại (`feature_connector_onboarding`, Draft) = mỗi host **BYO** (SePay OAuth / ApiPay api_key). Để **launch nhanh**, dùng **quan hệ enterprise với APIpay** ở cấp platform để **auto-provision** webhook/bank-account cho từng host (khỏi bắt host tự OAuth/nhập key).
docs/standards/decisions.md:362:- **Quyết định (chốt):** Platform giữ **APIpay enterprise credential**; onboarding host = platform gọi APIpay enterprise API đăng ký **per-host** bank-account + webhook `/webhook/apipay/:token`. **Tiền vẫn về tài khoản ngân hàng RIÊNG của host** — **money-custody KHÔNG đổi**, giữ invariant `feature_payment_reconcile` ("platform không giữ tiền player"). Routing tenant vẫn theo `:token → bank_accounts.webhook_token → tenant_id` (**BL-PAY-6**).
docs/standards/decisions.md-363-- **Lý do:** Giảm ma sát onboarding (self-serve chậm/khó với host phổ thông); tận dụng enterprise để auto-register. Vì không đảo money-custody nên **không** đụng hard rule / rủi ro pháp lý trung gian thanh toán.
docs/standards/decisions.md-364-- **Hệ quả:**
docs/standards/decisions.md:365:  - `feature_connector_onboarding(.md/_tech)` (Draft — sửa được): thêm path **"APIpay enterprise auto-provision"** (platform credential, per-host account/webhook), giữ BYO như fallback; provider vẫn `apipay`; verify HMAC `ApiPay-Signature` per-host secret không đổi.
docs/standards/decisions.md:366:  - **Bảo mật:** enterprise credential platform = tài sản KMS; lộ = ảnh hưởng nhiều tenant → cần rotation + audit riêng. Cần điều khoản enterprise (giới hạn số account/webhook, phí) từ APIpay.
docs/standards/decisions.md-367-  - **KHÔNG** đụng ledger/reconcile core — chỉ đường onboarding/provisioning.

docs/standards/decisions.md-370-

docs/standards/decisions.md-408-- **Bối cảnh:** Club Assistant đã chốt (DD-16..28) chỉ bao **KB per-CLB tenant-private**; trợ lý chỉ biết KB của đúng club. Owner (06/07) muốn trợ lý trả lời cả câu hỏi **về chính nền tảng Plexco** (tính năng, cách dùng, chính sách chung) — không có plane cho tri thức dùng chung không thuộc tenant nào.
docs/standards/decisions.md-409-- **Quyết định (đề xuất):** Thêm plane **GLOBAL read model** `platform_kb_*` (`platform_kb_sources/documents/chunks`) — **KHÔNG có `tenant_id`**, do process **platform-admin** ghi, role `app` chỉ **`GRANT SELECT`** (cô lập-bằng-grant như DD-12/`readonly`, KHÔNG vào RLS list `0009`). Mở rộng `searchKnowledgeBase(tenantCtx, query)` thành **union 2 nhánh**: `kb_chunks` WHERE tenant (RLS) ∪ `platform_kb_chunks` (global), hợp nhất RRF, gắn nhãn scope; **precedence club > platform** khi trùng chủ đề (`bot_configs.include_platform_kb`/`platform_kb_weight`).
docs/standards/decisions.md-410-- **Quan hệ với DD-21 (extension, KHÔNG "nguyên vẹn"):** DD-34 **mở rộng nguồn grounding** của DD-21: grounding vẫn **chỉ KB**, nhưng **allowed KB sources** mở từ `kb_chunks(tenant)` → `kb_chunks(tenant) ∪ platform_kb_chunks`. **Live-context (DD-28) vẫn tách riêng, không trộn.** Khi DD-34 ✅ **phải patch DD-21** + Club Assistant specs (`feature_club_assistant.md` bất biến "KB prompt = chỉ `kb_chunks`" · `BE/feature_club_assistant_tech.md` §5) để phản ánh nguồn union — đây là điều kiện promote, không được để DD-21 mâu thuẫn.
docs/standards/decisions.md-411-- **Lý do:** Tri thức nền tảng dùng chung mọi club → 1 nguồn, cập nhật đồng loạt (không nhồi-trùng-lặp N club); có tiền lệ bảng global trong feature (`embedding_cache`).
docs/standards/decisions.md:412:- **Cô lập & guard (bảng SELECT cho mọi tenant nên phải chặt):** thiếu `tenant_id` chỉ ngăn **routing/association** — **KHÔNG** tự ngăn nội dung tenant bị ingest **nhầm** vào `content`. Vì `platform_kb_*` đọc-chung mọi tenant, `platform_kb_*` **KHÔNG ĐƯỢC chứa** (không phải "không thể chứa") dữ liệu tenant/PII ⇒ cần guard nhiều lớp: (1) **source allowlist** — chỉ ingest tài liệu sản phẩm Plexco (platform-docs-only), không nhận nguồn tenant; (2) **admin review** trước khi `status='active'`; (3) **scanner + test bất biến** assert `content` không chứa tenant identifier/PII pattern (SĐT/email/`pay_code`/tên roster). Grant: role `app` chỉ SELECT; ghi bởi role platform-admin.
docs/standards/decisions.md-413-- **Hệ quả:** Test bất biến: platform table không `tenant_id`; role `app` không ghi được; **scanner PII/tenant-id trên `content` = xanh**; union không rò KB club A↔B; câu hỏi nền tảng nhận chunk platform. Chi tiết `proposals/plexco-global-kb-and-kb-autosync.md §3`. **Không** sửa spec Reviewed tới khi ✅ (khi ✅ mới patch DD-21 + Club Assistant specs).

docs/standards/environments-setup.md:59:| **SePay** — backend calls to `https://my.sepay.vn/api/v1` (OAuth2 or API token; transaction read, webhook and bank-account management) | **NO** | SePay Developer Docs: [Tạo API Token](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-api/v2/tao-api-token) · [OAuth 2.0 tổng quan](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/sepay-oauth2/tong-quan) · [Địa chỉ IP](https://developer.sepay.vn/vi/dia-chi-ip) | 2026-07-18 | API-token issuance (name + status only, tokens get full access) and the OAuth2 contract are fully specified with no source-IP registration or restriction step. The docs' only IP-allowlist page lists SePay's own egress IPs for merchants to allowlist SePay→merchant webhook delivery — the reverse direction — so inbound webhook hardening has no outbound-caller counterpart. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:60:| **ApiPay** — backend calls to `https://app.apipay.vn` (`/v2/client/banks`, `/v1/client/webhooks`) for DD-30 enterprise auto-provisioning and the BYO-key fallback | **NO** | ApiPay Docs: [Authentication](https://docs.apipay.vn/vi/api/authentication) — the site is client-rendered, so the full published docs corpus was retrieved and searched | 2026-07-18 | Authentication is fully specified as `Authorization: Bearer base64(accessKey:secretKey)` with keys issued in the ApiPay dashboard. The entire docs corpus contains no IP-allowlist, IP-registration or caller-IP-restriction concept; the only IP mentions are custom-domain DNS A-record instructions pointing at ApiPay's own server. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:61:| **Zalo Bot** — backend calls to `https://bot-api.zaloplatforms.com/bot<token>/<method>` (`sendMessage`, `setWebhook`, …) with the per-tenant bot token | **NO** | Zalo Bot Docs: [Xác thực](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/authorize/) · [Sử dụng API](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/call-api/) · [Bảng mã lỗi](https://bot.zapps.me/docs/error-code/) | 2026-07-18 | The complete calling contract is HTTPS plus the bot token as the sole credential (Telegram-style token-in-URL). Bot creation and authentication define no place to declare a caller IP, and the documented error model (400/401/403/404/408/429) has no origin/IP-denial class — a mandatory source-IP allowlist cannot be operated without such a mechanism. |
docs/standards/environments-setup.md:62:| **Meta Messenger** — backend `POST https://graph.facebook.com/v<ver>/<page_id>/messages` with `page_access_token` | **NO** | Meta: [Securing Graph API Requests](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/graph-api/securing-requests) | 2026-07-18 | Meta documents server-IP allowlisting as an opt-in app security control the developer may enable, alongside the optional `appsecret_proof` / "Require App Secret" hardening. By default Graph API calls are token-authenticated from any IP, so no fixed egress IP is required. |

docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-17-
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-18-**Có HAI quyết định, phải chốt theo thứ tự:**
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-19-
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-20-**QĐ-1 (lịch sử gate): posture private-network** — khi compute còn ở **Fly.io**, mọi managed DB nằm ở cloud khác nên **không có** private networking thật trừ khi PrivateLink (Supabase Team/Ent + AWS VPC) hoặc **co-locate compute vào cùng cloud DB**. Ba posture đã xét:
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md:21:- **(a) Public TLS + `sslmode=verify-full` + static-egress-IP allowlist** — giữ Fly, DB provider allowlist Fly egress IP. Đơn giản, giữ kiến trúc hiện tại. Câu chữ cũ ở `deployment-infra.md §6` ("Postgres KHÔNG bao giờ expose public") từng mâu thuẫn với posture này; rev 3 đã sync baseline thành "không public *unauthenticated*; truy cập qua TLS verify-full + IP allowlist".
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-22-- **(b) Strict private (PrivateLink/VPC)** — buộc chuyển deployment target khỏi Fly (vd sang AWS + Neon PrivateLink, hoặc co-locate DO). Đây là thay đổi hạ tầng, **KHÔNG đảo DD-10** vì DD-10 chỉ pin standalone container/không Vercel.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-23-
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-24-**QĐ-2 (provider), phụ thuộc QĐ-1:**
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-25-- Nếu **(a) public-TLS**: gate HA/SLA loại **Supabase Pro và Team** (đều không có uptime SLA). Team từ `$599` thêm AWS PrivateLink; uptime SLA chỉ ở Enterprise custom, còn auto-failover phải contract-confirm. Còn lại **DigitalOcean Managed** vs **Neon Scale**. → lịch sử phân tích nghiêng DigitalOcean.
--
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-38-| "Neon Launch không HA; HA chỉ ở Scale" | **Neon HA multi-AZ có ở MỌI plan** (storage multi-AZ mặc định, compute auto-reschedule AZ). **Scale** thêm **PrivateLink + 99.95% SLA + IP-allow rules + SOC2/HIPAA** — đó mới là thứ Scale-gated, không phải HA. |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-39-| "Neon always-on = ~1 CU ⇒ $120–160" | Tắt scale-to-zero **vẫn giữ autoscaling**, floor **0.25 CU**. Ước tính 1 CU thổi phồng chi phí Neon. |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-40-| "Supabase Pro là finalist đạt HA nhờ Pro + replica" | **Supabase Pro và Team KHÔNG đạt gate SLA.** Team từ `$599` thêm PrivateLink nhưng vẫn không có uptime SLA; uptime SLA chỉ ở Enterprise custom. Public material hiện tại chưa pin auto-failover theo plan, nên phải xác nhận hợp đồng. |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-41-| "Bảng scale cost theo tenant count" | Tenant count **không** map thẳng ra RAM/CU; Neon 1 CU chưa benchmark; Team $599 không bắt buộc chỉ vì DB lớn. Bảng cost hạ cấp thành **minh hoạ**, chỉ giữ anchor có nguồn (§5). |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md:42:| "IPv4 add-on Supabase mặc định cần" | Fly egress **IPv6** được; Supabase direct là IPv6 → **không mặc định cần IPv4 add-on** (chỉ cần khi allowlist bằng IPv4). |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-43-| Private network coi như đã lo ("Fly private networking / Supabase network restrictions") | Fly private networking chỉ nội-bộ-Fly; **DB managed ở cloud khác ⇒ không private thật** trừ PrivateLink/co-locate. **Gate chưa giải quyết** → §2. |

docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-53-3. **pg-boss + `LISTEN/NOTIFY`** cần connection session *luôn thức*.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-54-4. **Read isolation** cho discovery/public reads ngay Day-1; read-only node vật lý chỉ bật khi bounded pool/cache/circuit-breaker không đủ và telemetry chạm trigger.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-55-5. **HA = auto-failover** (không chỉ "warm standby") + **PITR** — vì hệ tiền. ⬅️ **gate cứng**.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-56-6. **Uptime SLA** cam kết — hệ tiền cần cam kết hợp đồng, không chỉ best-effort. ⬅️ **gate cứng**.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md:57:7. **Posture network:** DB không nhận traffic public *unauthenticated*; phải TLS verify-full + IP allowlist **tối thiểu**, private networking nếu khả thi. ⬅️ **QĐ-1**.
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-58-8. **Region Singapore** (VN latency).
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-59-9. **4 DB role** tách connection-level (least-privilege).
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-60-10. **Chi phí nhạy** (DD-29: host 0đ/250k) + **team nhỏ** (ít moving parts).
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-61-11. **Portability:** immutable OCI image + Postgres thuần + provider config nằm ngoài domain code; đổi provider vẫn cần rehearsal, logical replication/cutover, sequence/ledger reconciliation và performance gate — không được giản lược thành “đổi connection string”.
--
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-67-**Vấn đề:** compute (Fly.io) và DB managed (Supabase/Neon/DO) ở **cloud khác nhau** → traffic DB **không thể** đi private networking nội cloud. Ba đường:
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-68-
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-69-| Posture | Cách làm | Ưu | Nhược |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-70-|---|---|---|---|
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md:71:| **(a) Public TLS + verify-full + IP allowlist** | Cấp **Fly static egress IP** ($3.60/mo/region, IPv4); DB provider allowlist IP đó; ép `sslmode=verify-full` (**CA validation + hostname verification**) | Giữ Fly nguyên trạng; đơn giản; đủ mạnh cho phần lớn hệ tiền | Traffic qua public internet (đã mã hoá + IP-restricted); baseline `§6` đã sync, cần pin làm final nếu founder chọn (a) |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-72-| **(b) PrivateLink (AWS)** | Compute phải ở **AWS VPC**; dùng Neon PrivateLink / RDS | Private thật, không qua public | Rời Fly nhưng **không đảo DD-10**; phức tạp/đắt; Supabase PrivateLink = Team/Ent |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-73-| **(c) Co-locate cùng cloud** | Chuyển compute sang **DO** (Droplet/App Platform) + **DO Managed PG cùng VPC** | Private thật + rẻ + đơn giản trong một hệ | Rời Fly nhưng **không đảo DD-10**; egress-IP compatibility phải verify |

docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-80-
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-81-## 3. Gate HA + SLA — ai ĐẠT (dữ kiện rev 2)
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-82-
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-83-| Provider | Auto-failover HA | Uptime SLA | Private option (từ Fly) | Đạt gate? |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-84-|---|---|---|---|---|
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md:85:| **DigitalOcean Managed** | ✅ **standby node tự failover** (`$30.45` primary + `$30.45` standby, 2GB) | ✅ **99.95%** | Trusted-sources IP allowlist (public-TLS); VPC nếu co-locate DO | ✅ **ĐẠT** |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-86-| **Neon Scale** | ✅ HA multi-AZ (storage multi-AZ, compute auto-reschedule) | ✅ **99.95%** (Scale) | PrivateLink **AWS-only → KHÔNG dùng được từ Fly**; còn IP-allow + TLS | ✅ **ĐẠT** (nhưng private=chỉ IP-allow trên Fly) |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-87-| **Supabase Pro** | Public material không pin auto-failover theo Pro | ❌ **không uptime SLA ở Pro** | ❌ PrivateLink = Team/Ent | ❌ **KHÔNG đạt ở Pro** |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-88-| **Supabase Team / Enterprise** | **Cần contract confirmation** cho auto-failover | ❌ Team không có uptime SLA; ✅ Enterprise có SLA | ✅ PrivateLink (AWS VPC) | Team từ **`$599`** vẫn rớt SLA gate; Enterprise custom, không cost-comparable và vẫn cần AWS |
docs/dd-04-hosting-decision-brief.md-89-| **Self-host VPS** | ⚙️ tự dựng (Patroni/pg_auto_failover) | ❌ tự chịu | VPC nếu cùng cloud | ❌ rủi ro (§6) |
